<div id="post-accountsaccount_idcampaigns">
  #### POST accounts/:account_id/campaigns[](#post-accounts-account-id-campaigns "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

创建与当前账户关联的新广告系列。

**注意**：每个账户默认最多可以有 200 个处于活动状态的广告系列。不过，非活动广告系列的数量没有限制。此上限可以提高到 8,000 个活动广告系列。要启用更高的上限，广告主必须向其 X 客户经理提出请求。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/campaigns`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| 名称 | 描述 |
| :--- | :--- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需* | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| funding&#95;instrument&#95;id  <br />*必需* | 在其下创建广告系列的资金工具标识符。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `lygyi` |
| name  <br />*必需* | 广告系列的名称。最大长度：255 个字符。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `demo` |
| budget&#95;optimization  <br />*可选* | 选择要应用的预算优化类型。<br /><br />类型: 枚举<br /><br />默认值: `CAMPAIGN`  <br />可能的取值: `CAMPAIGN`, `LINE_ITEM` |
| daily&#95;budget&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*有时必需* | 要分配给广告系列的每日预算金额。将使用与指定资金工具关联的货币。对于 USD，5.50 美元表示为 5500000。<br /><br />**注意**：该值应小于或等于 `total_budget_amount_local_micro`，并且对于大多数资金工具类型是必需的。<br /><br />类型: long<br /><br />示例: `5500000` |
| entity&#95;status  <br />*可选* | 广告系列状态。<br /><br />类型: 枚举<br /><br />默认值: `ACTIVE`  <br />可能的取值: `ACTIVE`, `DRAFT`, `PAUSED` |
| purchase&#95;order&#95;number  <br />*可选* | 预订参考号。使用此字段帮助进行发票对账。最大长度：50 个字符。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `D00805843` |
| standard&#95;delivery  <br />*可选* | 启用标准或加速投放。有关标准与加速投放的更多信息，请参阅 [Budget Pacing](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#budget-pacing)。仅当 `budget_optimization` 设置为 `CAMPAIGN` 时可用。<br /><br />类型: boolean<br /><br />默认值: `true`  <br />可能的取值: `true`, `false` |
| total&#95;budget&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*可选* | 要分配给广告系列的总预算金额。将使用与指定资金工具关联的货币。对于 USD，37.50 美元表示为 37500000。<br /><br />类型: long<br /><br />示例: `37500000` |

**示例请求[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`POST https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/campaigns?funding_instrument_id=lygyi&name=demo&daily_budget_amount_local_micro=140000000&entity_status=PAUSED&budget_optimization=CAMPIAGN&standard_delivery=false`

**示例响应[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "name": "demo",
          "budget_optimization": "CAMPAIGN",
          "daily_budget_amount_local_micro": 140000000,
          "funding_instrument_id": "lygyi",
          "standard_delivery": false,
          "entity_status": "PAUSED",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "name": "demo",
        "budget_optimization": "CAMPAIGN",
        "reasons_not_servable": [
          "PAUSED_BY_ADVERTISER",
          "INCOMPLETE"
        ],
        "servable": false,
        "purchase_order_number": null,
        "effective_status": "UNKNOWN",
        "daily_budget_amount_local_micro": 140000000,
        "funding_instrument_id": "lygyi",
        "duration_in_days": null,
        "standard_delivery": false,
        "total_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
        "id": "hwtbm",
        "entity_status": "PAUSED",
        "frequency_cap": null,
        "currency": "USD",
        "created_at": "2022-06-03T21:38:07Z",
        "updated_at": "2022-06-03T21:38:07Z",
        "deleted": false
      }
    }
```

<div id="post-batchaccountsaccount_idcampaigns">
  #### POST batch/accounts/:account_id/campaigns[](#post-batch-accounts-account-id-campaigns "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

允许通过一次请求批量创建新的[广告活动](#post-accounts-account-id-campaigns)。

**批量请求**

* 当前批量请求的最大数量为 40。
* 所有参数都在请求体中发送，并且必须使用 `application/json` 的 `Content-Type`。
* 批量请求作为一个整体，要么全部失败，要么全部成功，并且无论成功还是错误，所有 API 响应都会保留与初始请求相同的条目顺序。

**批量响应**

批量 API 响应返回一个有序的条目集合。除此之外，它们在结构上与相应的单条目端点完全相同。

**批量错误**

* 请求级错误（例如，超过最大批量大小）会在响应中的 `errors` 对象下显示。
* 条目级错误（例如，缺少必需的广告活动参数）会在响应中的 `operation_errors` 对象下显示。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/batch/accounts/:account_id/campaigns`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                 | Description                                                                             |
| :----------------------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| operation&#95;type  <br />*required* | 针对每个条目执行的操作类型。<br /><br />类型: enum<br /><br />可能的取值: `Create`, `Delete`, `Update`       |
| params  <br />*required*             | 一个 JSON 对象，包含广告活动对象的所有参数。有关必需和可选广告活动参数的列表，请参见[此处](#post-accounts-account-id-campaigns)。 |

**示例请求[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`POST 'Content-Type: application/json' https://ads-api.x.com/12/batch/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/campaigns`

```json
    [
      {
        "operation_type":"Create",
        "params":{
          "name":"batch campaigns",
          "funding_instrument_id":"lygyi",
          "daily_budget_amount_local_micro":140000000,
          "entity_status":"PAUSED",
          "budget_optimization":"CAMPAIGN"
        }
      }
    ]
```

**示例响应[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": [
        {
          "name": "batch campaigns",
          "budget_optimization": "CAMPAIGN",
          "reasons_not_servable": [
            "PAUSED_BY_ADVERTISER",
            "INCOMPLETE"
          ],
          "servable": false,
          "purchase_order_number": null,
          "effective_status": "UNKNOWN",
          "daily_budget_amount_local_micro": 140000000,
          "funding_instrument_id": "lygyi",
          "duration_in_days": null,
          "standard_delivery": false,
          "total_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
          "id": "8yn7m",
          "entity_status": "PAUSED",
          "frequency_cap": null,
          "currency": "USD",
          "created_at": "2022-06-03T21:38:07Z",
          "updated_at": "2022-06-03T21:38:07Z",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ],
      "request": [
        {
          "params": {
            "name": "batch campaigns",
            "funding_instrument_id": "lygyi",
            "daily_budget_amount_local_micro": 140000000,
            "entity_status": "PAUSED",
            "budget_optimization":"CAMPAIGN",
            "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
          },
          "operation_type": "Create"
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="put-accountsaccount_idcampaignscampaign_id">
  #### PUT accounts/:account_id/campaigns/:campaign_id[](#put-accounts-account-id-campaigns-campaign-id "此标题的固定链接")
</div>

更新与当前账户关联的指定 campaign。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "此标题的固定链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/campaigns/:campaign_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "此标题的固定链接")**

| 名称 | 描述 |
| :--- | :--- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需* | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是所有 Advertiser API 请求（[GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 除外）的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| campaign&#95;id  <br />*必需* | 你在该请求中要操作的 campaign。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `8wku2` |
| budget&#95;optimization  <br />*可选* | 选择要应用的预算优化类型。<br /><br />类型: enum<br /><br />默认值: `CAMPAIGN`  <br />可能的取值: `CAMPAIGN`, `LINE_ITEM` |
| daily&#95;budget&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*可选* | 要分配给该 campaign 的每日预算金额。将使用与指定资金工具关联的货币。对于 USD，$5.50 表示为 5500000。如果未提供，campaign 将根据总预算和 campaign 投放周期平均投放。<br /><br />**注意**: 此值应小于或等于 `total_budget_amount_local_micro`。<br /><br />类型: long<br /><br />示例: `5500000` |
| entity&#95;status  <br />*可选* | campaign 的状态。<br /><br />类型: enum<br /><br />可能的取值: `ACTIVE`, `PAUSED` |
| name  <br />*可选* | campaign 的名称。最大长度：255 个字符。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `demo` |
| purchase&#95;order&#95;number  <br />*可选* | 预订参考编号。使用此字段帮助对发票进行核对与对账。最大长度：50 个字符。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `D00805843` |
| standard&#95;delivery  <br />*可选* | 启用标准或加速投放。有关标准与加速投放的更多信息，请参见 [Budget Pacing](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#budget-pacing)。仅当 `budget_optimization` 设置为 `CAMPAIGN` 时可用。<br /><br />类型: boolean<br /><br />默认值: `true`  <br />可能的取值: `true`, `false` |
| total&#95;budget&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*可选* | 要分配给该 campaign 的总预算金额。将使用与指定资金工具关联的货币。对于 USD，$37.50 表示为 37500000。<br /><br />类型: long<br /><br />示例: `140000000` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "此标题的固定链接")**

`PUT https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/campaigns/8wku2?total_budget_amount_local_micro=140000000`

**Example Response[](#example-response "此标题的固定链接")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "campaign_id": "8wku2",
          "daily_budget_amount_local_micro": 140000000,
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },

        "name": "test",
        "budget_optimization": "CAMPAIGN",

        "servable": false,

        "effective_status": "UNKNOWN",
        "daily_budget_amount_local_micro": 140000000,
        "funding_instrument_id": "lygyi",
```

删除当前账户下指定的广告系列（campaign）。

**注意**：删除 campaign 后无法恢复，之后再次尝试删除该资源将返回 HTTP 404。

| :-------------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需*  | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 以外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证的用户关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`18ce54d4x5t` |
| campaign&#95;id  <br />*必需* | 在请求中要操作的广告系列（campaign）的标识符。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`8yn7m`                                                                                                               |

<div id="get-content_categories">
  #### GET content_categories[](#get-content-categories "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

请求可作为广告单元的 `targeting_criteria` 进行设置的有效内容 `categories`。

每个 `content_category` 都映射到一个或多个 [IAB Categories](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#iab-categories)。要实现这一点，可以在批量 `targeting_critera` 端点上将 `targeting_type` 设置为 `IAB_CATEGORY`，以包含 `content_categories` 请求返回的相应 `iab_categories` 集合。否则将会导致验证错误。

可以使用 [GET publishers](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#publishers) 端点检索这些内容类别各自的发布者详细信息。

更多详细信息请参阅 [Video Views Pre-roll Objective Guide](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#video-views-preroll-objective)。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/content_categories`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

无请求参数

**示例请求[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

  ### 精选分类
</div>

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idcurated_categories">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/curated_categories[](#get-accounts-account-id-curated-categories "该标题的永久链接")
</div>

检索给定 `country_codes` 可用的推荐类别列表

每个 `curated_category` 仅在特定国家/地区可用，这些国家/地区由响应中的 `country_codes` 指定。

更多详细信息请参阅[视频预播（Pre-roll）观看量目标指南](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#video-views-preroll-objective)。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "该标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/curated_categories`

**参数[](#parameters "该标题的永久链接")**

| 名称 | 描述 |
| :--- | :--- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required* | 使用中的账户标识符。出现在资源路径中，通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已通过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| country&#95;codes  <br />*required* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的两个字母 ISO 国家/地区代码列表，将响应限定为仅包含所需的国家/地区。最多可提供 200 个国家/地区代码。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `US` |
| cursor  <br />*optional* | 指定一个游标以获取下一页结果。有关更多信息，请参阅[分页](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `8x7v00oow` |

**示例请求[](#example-request "该标题的永久链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/curated_categories?country_codes=US`

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idcurated_categoriescurated_category_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/curated_categories/:curated_category_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-curated-categories-curated-category-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索特定 `curated_category_id` 的详细信息

每个 `curated_category` 仅在特定国家/地区可用，这些国家/地区由响应中的 `country_codes` 指定。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/curated_categories/:curated_category_id`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                    | Description                                                                                                                                                                           |
| :-------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需*              | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| curated&#95;category&#95;id  <br />*必需* | 你在请求中要操作的精选类别（Curated Category）的引用。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `9ddrgesiap6o`                                                                                               |

**示例请求[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/curated_categories/9ddrgesiap6o`

**示例响应[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "id": "9ddrgesiap6o",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "name": "Soccer",
        "description": "Run next to the best of everyday soccer content including college teams, professional teams, and the top sports media handles sharing major soccer coverage.",
        "country_codes": [],
        "publisher_user_ids": [
```

<div id="features">
  ### 功能
</div>

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  使用 Postman 运行
</Button>

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idfeatures">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/features[](#get-accounts-account-id-features "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

获取此广告账户已获授并可访问的功能集合。功能通过描述性的功能键进行标识，只有当这些功能以测试版或其他受限发布形式引入并在 Ads API 中可用时，才会通过此端点返回。不符合此条件的功能不会通过此端点返回。

**注意**：此端点旨在通过提升对客户端测试版访问情况的可见性，帮助 Ads API 生态系统开发。API 开发者不能代表广告主请求访问功能，此类请求只能由广告主向其 X 客户经理提出。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/features`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                         | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| :--------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需*   | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: 字符串<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t`                        |
| feature&#95;keys  <br />*可选* | 一个可选参数，用于查询特定功能键。请求中可以包含多个以逗号分隔的键。<br /><br />**注意**：只有此账户可访问的功能才会包含在响应中。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `REACH_AND_FREQUENCY_ANALYTICS`, `REACH_FREQUENCY_CAP`, `WEBSITE_CLICKS_CPM_BILLING` |

**示例请求[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/features`

**示例响应[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": [
        "CITY_TARGETING",
        "CONVERSATION_CARD",
        "PROMOTED_MEDIA_POLLS",
        "REACH_AND_FREQUENCY_ANALYTICS",
        "REACH_FREQUENCY_CAP",
        "UNIVERSAL_LOOKALIKE"
      ]
    }
```

<div id="post-accountsaccount_idfeatures">
  #### POST accounts/:account_id/features[](#post-accounts-account-id-features "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

**仅 SANDBOX 环境可用**

向 sandbox 账户添加功能。

可以通过 [GET accounts/:account&#95;id/features](#get-accounts-account-id-features) 端点获取最新的账户功能列表。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api-sandbox.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/features`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                               | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| :--------------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*   | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已通过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`gq180y`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| feature&#95;keys  <br />*required* | 以逗号分隔的账户功能列表，要添加到该账户中。<br /><br />类型：枚举<br /><br />可能的取值：`AGE_TARGETING`, `ALLOW_SKIPPABLE_VIDEOS_FOR_PREROLL_VIEWS_OBJECTIVE`, `AWARENESS_OBJECTIVE`, `BRAND_TPN`, `CHARGE_FOR_GOOD_CLICK`, `CONVERSATION_CARD`, `CONVERSATION_CARD_FOUR_OPTIONS`, `CONVERSATION_CARD_UNLOCK`, `CPI_CHARGING`, `DIRECT_MESSAGE_CARD`, `DR_TAP`, `ENGAGER_RETARGETING`, `EVENT_TARGETING`, `INSTALLED_APP_CATEGORY_TARGETING`, `MOBILE_CONVERSION_TRANSACTION_VALUE`, `OPTIMIZED_ACTION_BIDDING`, `REACH_AND_FREQUENCY_ANALYTICS`, `REACH_FREQUENCY_CAP`, `VALIDATED_AGE_TARGETING`, `VIDEO_VIEWS_MIDROLL_OBJECTIVE`, `PREROLL_VIEWS_OBJECTIVE`, `VIDEO_APP_DOWNLOAD_CARD` |

**示例请求[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`POST https://ads-api-sandbox.x.com/12/accounts/gq180y/features?feature_keys=VALIDATED_AGE_TARGETING`

**示例响应[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "account_id": "gq180y",
          "feature_keys": [
            "VALIDATED_AGE_TARGETING"
          ]
        }
      },
      "data": [
        "ALLOW_SKIPPABLE_VIDEOS_FOR_PREROLL_VIEWS_OBJECTIVE",
        "AWARENESS_OBJECTIVE",
        "CPI_CHARGING",
        "EVENT_TARGETING",
        "INSTALLED_APP_CATEGORY_TARGETING",
        "MOBILE_CONVERSION_TRANSACTION_VALUE",
        "OPTIMIZED_ACTION_BIDDING",
        "VALIDATED_AGE_TARGETING",
        "VIDEO_APP_DOWNLOAD_CARD"
      ]
    }
```

<div id="delete-accountsaccount_idfeatures">
  #### DELETE accounts/:account_id/features[](#delete-accounts-account-id-features "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

**仅限 SANDBOX 环境**

从 sandbox 账户中移除某项功能。

最新的账户功能列表可以通过 [GET accounts/:account&#95;id/features](#get-accounts-account-id-features) 端点获取。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api-sandbox.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/features`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| 名称                           | 描述                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| :--------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需*   | 所使用广告账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `gq180y`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| feature&#95;keys  <br />*必需* | 要从账户中移除的账户功能的以逗号分隔的列表。<br /><br />类型: 枚举<br /><br />可能的取值: `AGE_TARGETING`, `ALLOW_SKIPPABLE_VIDEOS_FOR_PREROLL_VIEWS_OBJECTIVE`, `AWARENESS_OBJECTIVE`, `BRAND_TPN`, `CHARGE_FOR_GOOD_CLICK`, `CONVERSATION_CARD`, `CONVERSATION_CARD_FOUR_OPTIONS`, `CONVERSATION_CARD_UNLOCK`, `CPI_CHARGING`, `DIRECT_MESSAGE_CARD`, `DR_TAP`, `ENGAGER_RETARGETING`, `EVENT_TARGETING`, `INSTALLED_APP_CATEGORY_TARGETING`, `MOBILE_CONVERSION_TRANSACTION_VALUE`, `OPTIMIZED_ACTION_BIDDING`, `REACH_AND_FREQUENCY_ANALYTICS`, `REACH_FREQUENCY_CAP`, `VALIDATED_AGE_TARGETING`, `VIDEO_VIEWS_MIDROLL_OBJECTIVE`, `PREROLL_VIEWS_OBJECTIVE`, `VIDEO_APP_DOWNLOAD_CARD` |

**请求示例[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`DELETE https://ads-api-sandbox.x.com/12/accounts/gq180y/features?feature_keys=PREROLL_VIEWS_OBJECTIVE`

**响应示例[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "account_id": "gq180y",
          "feature_keys": [
            "PREROLL_VIEWS_OBJECTIVE"
          ]
        }
      },
      "data": [
        "CPI_CHARGING",
        "EVENT_TARGETING",
        "INSTALLED_APP_CATEGORY_TARGETING",
        "MOBILE_CONVERSION_TRANSACTION_VALUE",
        "OPTIMIZED_ACTION_BIDDING",
        "VIDEO_APP_DOWNLOAD_CARD"
      ]
    }
```

### 资金来源

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  在 Postman 中运行
</Button>

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idfunding_instruments">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/funding_instruments[](#get-accounts-account-id-funding-instruments "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索与当前账户关联的部分或全部资金工具的详细信息。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/funding_instruments`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| 名称                                         | 描述                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| :----------------------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需*                 | 广告账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与经过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `18ce54d4x5t`                         |
| count  <br />*可选*                          | 指定每个独立请求尝试检索的记录数。<br /><br />类型: int<br /><br />默认值: `200`  <br />最小值、最大值: `1`, `1000`                                                                                                                           |
| cursor  <br />*可选*                         | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。有关更多信息，请参阅 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `8x7v00oow`                                                                                                |
| funding&#95;instrument&#95;ids  <br />*可选* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应限定为仅包含所需的资金工具。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `lygyi`                                                                                                                    |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*可选*                    | 按支持的属性进行升序或降序排序。有关更多信息，请参阅 [Sorting](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `created_at-asc`                                                                                             |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*可选*               | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />类型: boolean<br /><br />默认值: `false`  <br />可能的取值: `true`, `false`                                                                                                                       |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*可选*       | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**注意**: 此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**注意**: 包含 `total_count` 的请求将具有更低的速率限制，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />类型: boolean<br /><br />默认值: `false`  <br />可能的取值: `true`, `false` |

**示例请求[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/funding_instruments`

**示例响应[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "start_time": "2016-07-22T04:24:04Z",
          "description": "Visa ending in 0650",
          "credit_limit_local_micro": 200000000,
          "end_time": null,
          "id": "lygyi",
          "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t",
          "reasons_not_able_to_fund": [],
          "io_header": null,
          "currency": "USD",
          "funded_amount_local_micro": 645940000,
          "created_at": "2016-07-22T04:24:04Z",
          "type": "CREDIT_CARD",
          "able_to_fund": true,
          "updated_at": "2017-04-05T00:25:13Z",
          "credit_remaining_local_micro": null,
          "deleted": false
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idfunding_instrumentsfunding_instrument_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/funding_instruments/:funding_instrument_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-funding-instruments-funding-instrument-id "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

获取与当前账户关联的特定资金工具。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/funding_instruments/:id`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                                            | Description                                                                                                                                                                     |
| :---------------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*                | 杠杆账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`18ce54d4x5t` |
| funding&#95;instrument&#95;id  <br />*required* | 你在此请求中要操作的资金工具的引用。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`lygyi`                                                                                                                      |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*              | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />类型：布尔值<br /><br />默认值：`false`  <br />可能的取值：`true`、`false`                                                                                              |

**示例请求[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/funding_instruments/lygyi`

**示例响应[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "funding_instrument_id": "lygyi",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "start_time": "2016-07-22T04:24:04Z",
        "description": "Visa ending in 0650",
        "credit_limit_local_micro": 200000000,
        "end_time": null,
        "id": "lygyi",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t",
        "reasons_not_able_to_fund": [],
        "io_header": null,
        "currency": "USD",
        "funded_amount_local_micro": 645940000,
        "created_at": "2016-07-22T04:24:04Z",
        "type": "CREDIT_CARD",
        "able_to_fund": true,
        "updated_at": "2017-04-05T00:25:13Z",
        "credit_remaining_local_micro": null,
        "deleted": false
      }
    }
```

<div id="post-accountsaccount_idfunding_instruments">
  #### POST accounts/:account_id/funding_instruments[](#post-accounts-account-id-funding-instruments "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

**仅限沙箱环境**

在沙箱环境中创建资金工具。

在使用沙箱资金工具时不存在产生费用的风险。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api-sandbox.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/funding_instruments`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| 名称                                                | 描述                                                                                                                                                                         |
| :------------------------------------------------ | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需*                        | 杠杆账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`gq1844` |
| currency  <br />*必需*                              | 货币，使用 [ISO-4217](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_4217) 表示。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`USD`                                                                         |
| start&#95;time  <br />*必需*                        | 资金工具开始生效并可用的日期，使用 [ISO 8601](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_8601) 表示。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`2017-05-19T07:00:00Z`                                            |
| type  <br />*必需*                                  | 要创建的资金工具的类型。<br /><br />类型：枚举<br /><br />可能的取值：`AGENCY_CREDIT_LINE`, `CREDIT_CARD`, `CREDIT_LINE`, `INSERTION_ORDER`, `PARTNER_MANAGED`                                    |
| end&#95;time  <br />*有时必需*                        | 资金工具失效的日期，使用 [ISO 8601](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_8601) 表示。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`2017-05-26T07:00:00Z`                                                 |
| credit&#95;limit&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*可选*  | 此资金工具可用的总信用额度。<br /><br />**注意**：仅适用于某些资金工具类型。<br /><br />类型：long<br /><br />示例：`37500000`                                                                                 |
| funded&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*可选* | 分配给此资金工具的预算总额。<br /><br />**注意**：仅适用于某些资金工具类型。<br /><br />类型：long<br /><br />示例：`37500000`                                                                                 |

**示例请求[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`POST https://ads-api-sandbox.x.com/12/accounts/gq1844/funding_instruments?currency=USD&start_time=2017-07-10T00:00:00Z&type=INSERTION_ORDER&end_time=2018-01-10T00:00:00Z&funded_amount_local_micro=140000000000`

**示例响应[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": {
        "start_time": "2017-07-10T00:00:00Z",
        "description": "(尚未设置支付方式)",
        "credit_limit_local_micro": null,
        "end_time": "2018-01-10T00:00:00Z",
        "id": "hxtet",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "account_id": "gq1844",
        "reasons_not_able_to_fund": [],
        "io_header": null,
        "currency": "USD",
        "funded_amount_local_micro": 140000000000,
        "created_at": "2017-09-09T05:23:28Z",
        "type": "INSERTION_ORDER",
        "able_to_fund": true,
        "updated_at": "2017-09-09T05:23:28Z",
        "credit_remaining_local_micro": null,
        "deleted": false
      },
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "start_time": "2017-07-10T00:00:00Z",
          "end_time": "2018-01-10T00:00:00Z",
          "account_id": "gq1844",
          "currency": "USD",
          "funded_amount_local_micro": 140000000000,
          "type": "INSERTION_ORDER"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="delete-accountsaccount_idfunding_instrumentsfunding_instrument_id">
  #### DELETE accounts/:account_id/funding_instruments/:funding_instrument_id[](#delete-accounts-account-id-funding-instruments-funding-instrument-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

**仅限 SANDBOX 环境**

在 sandbox 环境中删除资金工具。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api-sandbox.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/funding_instruments/:funding_instrument_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                            | Description                                                                                                                                                                            |
| :---------------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*                | 目标账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `gq1844` |
| funding&#95;instrument&#95;id  <br />*required* | 你在请求中要操作的资金工具的标识。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Exampple: `hxt82`                                                                                                                 |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`DELETE https://ads-api-sandbox.x.com/12/accounts/gq1844/funding_instruments/hxt82`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": {
        "start_time": "2017-08-30T19:23:47Z",
        "description": "(尚未设置付款方式)",
        "credit_limit_local_micro": 500000000,
        "end_time": null,
        "id": "hxt82",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "account_id": "gq1844",
        "reasons_not_able_to_fund": [
          "DELETED"
        ],
        "io_header": null,
        "currency": "USD",
        "funded_amount_local_micro": null,
        "created_at": "2017-08-30T19:23:47Z",
        "type": "CREDIT_CARD",
        "able_to_fund": false,
        "updated_at": "2017-09-09T02:08:30Z",
        "credit_remaining_local_micro": null,
        "deleted": true
      },
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "funding_instrument_id": "hxt82",
          "account_id": "gq1844"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="iab-categories">
  ### IAB 分类
</div>

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  在 Postman 中运行
</Button>

<div id="get-iab_categories">
  #### GET iab_categories[](#get-iab-categories "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

请求适用于广告组（`line_items`）的有效应用 `categories`。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/iab_categories`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                       | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| :----------------------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| count  <br />*optional*                    | 指定每个独立请求尝试检索的记录数量。<br /><br />类型: int<br /><br />默认值: `200`  <br />最小值、最大值: `1`、`1000`                                                                                                                     |
| cursor  <br />*optional*                   | 指定用于获取下一页分类的游标。更多信息请参阅 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `gc-ddf4a`                                                                                              |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*optional* | 是否包含 `total_count` 响应属性。<br /><br />**注意**：此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**注意**：包含 `total_count` 的请求其速率限制更低，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />类型: boolean<br /><br />默认值: `false`  <br />可能的取值: `true`、`false` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/iab_categories?count=2`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": [
        {
          "id": "IAB1",
          "parent_id": null,
          "name": "Arts & Entertainment"
        },
        {
          "id": "IAB1-1",
          "parent_id": "IAB1",
          "name": "Books & Literature"
        }
      ],
      "next_cursor": "uxa8",
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "count": 2
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="line-items">
  ### 投放单元
</div>

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  在 Postman 中运行
</Button>

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idline_items">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/line_items[](#get-accounts-account-id-line-items "跳转到此标题的永久链接")
</div>

检索与当前账户关联的部分或全部 line item 的详细信息。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "跳转到此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/line_items`

**参数[](#parameters "跳转到此标题的永久链接")**

| Name | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required* | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已验证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| campaign&#95;ids  <br />*optional* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应限定为特定推广活动下的 line item。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8gdx6` |
| count  <br />*optional* | 指定每个独立请求尝试检索的记录数。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `200`  <br />Min, Max: `1`, `1000` |
| cursor  <br />*optional* | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。有关更多信息，请参阅 [分页](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8x7v00oow` |
| funding&#95;instrument&#95;ids  <br />*optional* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应限定为特定资金工具下的 line item。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `lygyi` |
| line&#95;item&#95;ids  <br />*optional* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应限定为所需的 line item。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8v7jo` |
| q  <br />*optional* | 一个可选查询，用于按 `name` 限定资源范围。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Min, Max length: `1`, `255` |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*optional* | 按支持的属性以升序或降序排序。有关更多信息，请参阅 [排序](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `created_at-asc` |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional* | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |
| with&#95;draft  <br />*optional* | 在请求中包含草稿状态推广活动的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*optional* | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**注意**：此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**注意**：包含 `total_count` 的请求具有更低的速率限制，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |

**示例请求[](#example-request "跳转到此标题的永久链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/line_items?line_item_ids=itttx`

**示例响应[](#example-response "跳转到此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t",
          "line_item_ids": [
            "itttx"
          ]
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "advertiser_user_id": "756201191646691328",
          "name": "li-18",
          "placements": [
            "ALL_ON_TWITTER"
          ],
          "start_time": "2021-02-16T00:00:00Z",
          "bid_amount_local_micro": 320000,
          "advertiser_domain": null,
          "target_cpa_local_micro": null,
          "primary_web_event_tag": null,
          "goal": "ENGAGEMENT",
          "daily_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
          "product_type": "PROMOTED_TWEETS",
          "end_time": null,
          "funding_instrument_id": "lygyi",
          "bid_strategy": "MAX",
          "duration_in_days": null,
          "standard_delivery": null,
          "total_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
          "objective": "ENGAGEMENTS",
          "id": "itttx",
          "entity_status": "PAUSED",
          "automatic_tweet_promotion": null,
          "frequency_cap": null,
          "android_app_store_identifier": null,
          "categories": [],
          "currency": "USD",
          "pay_by": "ENGAGEMENT",
          "created_at": "2021-02-23T23:37:54Z",
          "ios_app_store_identifier": null,
          "updated_at": "2022-06-01T02:01:18Z",
          "campaign_id": "f4z6x",
          "creative_source": "MANUAL",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idline_itemsline_item_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/line_items/:line_item_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-line-items-line-item-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

获取当前账户关联的特定广告项目（line item）。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/line_items/:line_item_id`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| 名称                               | 描述                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| :------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需*       | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 外，一般是所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与经过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`18ce54d4x5t` |
| line&#95;item&#95;id  <br />*必需* | 本次请求中要操作的广告项目（line item）的标识引用。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`8v7jo`                                                                                                               |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*可选*     | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />类型：布尔值<br /><br />默认值：`false`  <br />可能的取值：`true`、`false`                                                                                                   |

**示例请求[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/line_items/itttx`

**示例响应[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "line_item_id": "itttx",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "advertiser_user_id": "756201191646691328",
        "name": "li-18",
        "placements": [
          "ALL_ON_TWITTER"
        ],
        "start_time": "2021-02-16T00:00:00Z",
        "bid_amount_local_micro": 320000,
        "advertiser_domain": null,
        "target_cpa_local_micro": null,
        "primary_web_event_tag": null,
        "goal": "ENGAGEMENT",
        "daily_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
        "product_type": "PROMOTED_TWEETS",
        "end_time": null,
        "funding_instrument_id": "lygyi",
        "bid_strategy": "MAX",
        "duration_in_days": null,
        "standard_delivery": null,
        "total_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
        "objective": "ENGAGEMENTS",
        "id": "itttx",
        "entity_status": "PAUSED",
        "automatic_tweet_promotion": null,
        "frequency_cap": null,
        "android_app_store_identifier": null,
        "categories": [],
        "currency": "USD",
        "pay_by": "ENGAGEMENT",
        "created_at": "2021-02-23T23:37:54Z",
        "ios_app_store_identifier": null,
        "updated_at": "2022-06-01T02:01:18Z",
        "campaign_id": "f4z6x",
        "creative_source": "MANUAL",
        "deleted": false
      }
    }
```

<div id="post-accountsaccount_idline_items">
  #### POST accounts/:account_id/line_items[](#post-accounts-account-id-line-items "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

创建一个与当前帐户中指定 campaign 关联的 line item。

同一 campaign 中的所有 line item 必须具有相同的 `product_type` 和 `objective`。

当使用 `PROMOTED_ACCOUNT` 产品类型时，将一条推文与 `line_item` 关联，除了标准的 `PROMOTED_ACCOUNT` 展示位置之外，还会在移动端时间线中增加展示位置。

设置 `android_app_store_identifier` 或 `ios_app_store_identifier` 之一，会自动为与所推广移动应用匹配的 line item 添加定向条件；例如，传入 `ios_app_store_identifier` 会为 `iOS` 添加 `PLATFORM` [定向条件](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#targeting-options)。

**注意**：每个 campaign 最多可以有 100 个 line item，且在所有 campaign 中最多可以有 256 个处于活动状态的 line item。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/line_items`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| 名称                                                            | 描述                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| :------------------------------------------------------------ | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需*                                    | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求中的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例: `18ce54d4x5t`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| campaign&#95;id  <br />*必需*                                   | 用于在其下创建推广线项的广告系列标识符。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `8slvg`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| end&#95;time  <br />*必需*                                      | 推广单元将停止投放的时间，以 [ISO 8601](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_8601) 格式表示。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `2017-10-05T00:00:00Z`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| 投放目标  <br />*必需*                                              | 此 line item 所对应的广告活动目标。<br /><br />类型: enum<br /><br />可能的取值: `APP_ENGAGEMENTS`, `APP_INSTALLS`, `REACH`, `FOLLOWERS`, `ENGAGEMENTS`, `VIDEO_VIEWS`, `PREROLL_VIEWS`, `WEBSITE_CLICKS`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| placements  <br />*必需*                                        | 此投放项的展示位置。指定一个以逗号分隔的投放位置值列表。<br /><br />类型: enum<br /><br />可能的取值: `ALL_ON_TWITTER`, `PUBLISHER_NETWORK`, `TAP_BANNER`, `TAP_FULL`, `TAP_FULL_LANDSCAPE`, `TAP_NATIVE`, `TAP_MRECT`,`TWITTER_PROFILE`, `TWITTER_REPLIES`, `TWITTER_SEARCH`, `TWITTER_TIMELINE`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| product&#95;type  <br />*必需*                                  | 此订单项所包含的推广产品类型。<br /><br />类型：enum<br /><br />可能的取值：`MEDIA`, `PROMOTED_ACCOUNT`, `PROMOTED_TWEETS`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| start&#95;time  <br />*必需*                                    | 该 line item 的开始投放时间，以 [ISO 8601](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_8601) 格式表示。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `2017-07-05T00:00:00Z`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| advertiser&#95;domain  <br />*有时为必需*                          | 此广告主的网站域名，不包含协议部分。<br /><br />**注意**：当该 line item 的投放位置设置为 `PUBLISHER_NETWORK` 时为必需。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`x.com`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| android&#95;app&#95;store&#95;identifier  <br />*有时必需*        | 用于推广应用的 Google Play 商店标识符。<br /><br />**注意**：当广告目标为 `APP_INSTALLS` 或 `APP_ENGAGEMENTS` 时，必须至少设置一个应用商店标识符，即 `android_app_store_identifier` 或 `ios_app_store_identifier` 之一。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`com.twitter.android`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| bid&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*有时必需*              | 与此订单项关联的出价金额。将使用与指定资金工具关联的货币。对于美元 (USD)，$5.50 表示为 5500000。<br /><br />**注意**：当 `bid_strategy` 设置为 `MAX` 或 `TARGET` 时为必需。<br /><br />**注意**：仅接受大于零的值。<br /><br />类型: long<br /><br />示例: `5500000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| categories  <br />*有时必需*                                      | 与该广告主相关的 IAB 类别。参见 [GET iab&#95;categories](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#iab-categories)。<br /><br />**注意**：当该 line item 的投放位置设为 `PUBLISHER_NETWORK` 时为必需。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`IAB3-1`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ios&#95;app&#95;store&#95;identifier  <br />*有时必需*            | 用于推广应用的 Apple App Store 标识符中的数字部分。<br /><br />**注意**：当广告目标为 `APP_INSTALLS` 或 `APP_ENGAGEMENTS` 时，必须至少设置一个应用商店标识符——`android_app_store_identifier` 或 `ios_app_store_identifier` 之一。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `333903271`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| primary&#95;web&#95;event&#95;tag  <br />*有时是必需的*             | 主要 Web 事件标签的标识符。可对与此订单项对应的广告系列互动进行更精确的跟踪。<br /><br />**注意**：当订单项的目标设置为 `WEBSITE_CONVERSIONS` 时是必需的。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `nvo4z`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| advertiser&#95;user&#95;id  <br />*可选*                        | 用于推广 `PREROLL_VIEWS` 广告的账号对应的 X 用户标识符。只有部分客户端应用可以使用该参数。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`312226591`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| audience&#95;expansion  <br />*可选*                            | 用于通过定位与现有目标用户相似的用户来扩大广告活动的覆盖范围。<br /><br />**注意**：默认情况下不会进行任何扩展。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `BROAD`, `DEFINED`, `EXPANDED`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| bid&#95;strategy  <br />*可选*                                  | 竞价机制。<br /><br />`AUTO` 会根据每日预算和广告活动投放日期自动优化出价。<br /><br />`MAX` 设定允许的最高出价，当目标设置为 `REACH` 或 `FOLLOWERS` 时**不可用**。<br /><br />`TARGET` 旨在使每日平均出价保持在指定的 `bid_amount_local_micro` 的 20% 以内，并且当目标设置为 `REACH`、`FOLLOWERS` 或 `WEBSITE_CLICKS` 时可用。<br /><br />**注意**：如果设置为 `AUTO`，则会忽略 `bid_amount_local_micro`。<br /><br />**注意**：默认值取决于目标。<br /><br />类型: 枚举类型<br /><br />可能的取值: `AUTO`, `MAX`, `TARGET`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| duration&#95;in&#95;days  <br />*可选*                          | 达到 `frequency_cap` 所对应的时间周期（天数）。<br /><br />类型: int<br /><br />可能的取值: `1`, `7`, `30`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| entity&#95;status  <br />*可选*                                 | line item 的状态。<br /><br />类型: 枚举<br /><br />默认值: `ACTIVE`  <br />可能的取值: `ACTIVE`, `DRAFT`, `PAUSED`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| frequency&#95;cap  <br />*可选*                                 | 向同一用户展示广告的最大次数。<br /><br />**注意**：仅支持用于 `REACH`、`ENGAGEMENTS`、`VIDEO_VIEWS` 和 `PREROLL_VIEWS` 投放目标。<br /><br />类型：int<br /><br />示例：`5`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| goal  <br />*可选*                                              | 用于此 line item 的优化设置。<br /><br />`APP_PURCHASES` 选项可用于 `APP_INSTALL` 目标。`APP_CLICKS` 和 `APP_INSTALLS` 选项可用于 `APP_INSTALL` 和 `APP_ENGAGEMENTS` 两种目标，并且可能需要使用受支持的 [MACT 合作伙伴](https://business.x.com/en/help/campaign-setup/create-an-app-installs-or-app-engagement-campaign/mobile-app-conversion-tracking.html)。<br /><br />`SITE_VISITS` 选项仅可用于 `WEBSITE_CLICKS` 目标。<br /><br />**注意**：默认值取决于目标。<br /><br />类型：enum<br /><br />可能的取值：`APP_CLICKS`，`APP_INSTALLS`，`APP_PURCHASES`，`ENGAGEMENT`，`FOLLOWERS`，`LINK_CLICKS`，`MAX_REACH`，`PREROLL`，`PREROLL_STARTS`，`REACH_WITH_ENGAGEMENT`，`SITE_VISITS`，`VIDEO_VIEW`，`VIEW_3S_100PCT`，`VIEW_6S`，`VIEW_15S`，`WEBSITE_CONVERSIONS` |
| 名称  <br />*可选*                                                | 此广告项目的名称。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`demo`<br /><br />最小和最大长度：`1`，`255`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| pay&#95;by  <br />*可选*                                        | 对该订单项进行计费所依据的单位。此设置只能针对使用 `APP_INSTALLS` 目标的订单项进行修改。<br /><br />**注意**：默认的 `pay_by` 会根据广告系列目标和订单项的出价单位自动设置。<br /><br />`APP_INSTALLS` 目标支持 `APP_CLICK` 和 `IMPRESSION` 两种取值，其中 `IMPRESSION` 为默认值。<br /><br />`LINK_CLICKS` 目标支持 `LINK_CLICK` 和 `IMPRESSION` 两种取值，其中 `IMPRESSION` 为默认值，但在将 `bid_strategy` 设置为 `TARGET` 时不支持选择 `IMPRESSION`。<br /><br />`SITE_VISITS` 目标仅支持 `IMPRESSION` 取值。<br /><br />类型：枚举<br /><br />可能的取值：`APP_CLICK`, `IMPRESSION`, `LINK_CLICK`                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| standard&#95;delivery  <br />*可选*                             | 启用标准或加速投放。有关标准与加速投放的更多信息，请参见 [Budget Pacing](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#budget-pacing)。仅当父级广告系列的 `budget_optimization` 设置为 `LINE_ITEM` 时才可用<br /><br />类型: boolean<br /><br />默认值: `true`  <br />可能的取值: `true`, `false`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| total&#95;budget&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*可选*   | 要分配给该 line item 的总预算金额。将使用与指定资金工具关联的货币。对于 USD，$37.50 表示为 37500000。<br /><br />Type: long<br /><br />Example: `37500000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| daily&#95;budget&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*有时必需* | 要分配给广告系列的每日预算金额。将使用与指定资金来源关联的货币。对于 USD，$5.50 表示为 5500000。未提供时，广告系列会在整个投放周期内基于总预算平均分配支出。仅当父广告系列的 `budget_optimization` 设置为 `LINE_ITEM` 时可用。<br /><br />**注意**：该值应小于或等于 `total_budget_amount_local_micro`。<br /><br />类型: long<br /><br />示例: `5500000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

**示例请求[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`POST https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/line_items?campaign_id=hwtq0&objective=ENGAGEMENTS&product_type=PROMOTED_TWEETS&placements=ALL_ON_TWITTER&bid_amount_local_micro=3210000&entity_status=PAUSED&daily_budget_amount_local_micro=1000000&start_time=2022-06-15`

**示例响应[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**
