<div id="post-batchaccountsaccount_idline_items">
  #### POST batch/accounts/:account_id/line_items[](#post-batch-accounts-account-id-line-items "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

允许在单个请求中批量创建新的 [line items](#post-accounts-account-id-line-items)。

**批量请求**

* 当前最大批量为 40。
* 所有参数都在请求正文中发送，并且必须使用 `Content-Type` 为 `application/json`。
* 批量请求作为一个整体一起成功或失败，无论成功还是失败，所有 API 响应都会保留初始请求中项目的顺序。

**批量响应**

批量 API 响应返回有序的项目集合。除此之外，它们在结构上与相应的单个项目端点完全相同。

**批量错误**

* 请求级错误（例如超过最大批量大小）会在响应中的 `errors` 对象下显示。
* 项目级错误（例如缺少必需的 line item 参数）会在响应中的 `operation_errors` 对象下显示。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/batch/accounts/:account_id/line_items`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                 | Description                                                                                           |
| :----------------------------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| operation&#95;type  <br />*required* | 对每个项目执行的操作类型。<br /><br />Type: enum<br /><br />Possible values: `Create`, `Delete`, `Update`          |
| params  <br />*required*             | 一个包含 line item 对象所有参数的 JSON 对象。有关必需和可选 line item 参数的列表，请参见[此处](#post-accounts-account-id-line-items)。 |

**示例请求[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`POST 'Content-Type: application/json' https://ads-api.x.com/12/batch/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/line_items`

```json
    [
      {
        "operation_type":"Create",
        "params":{
          "campaign_id":"8yn7m",
          "objective":"ENGAGEMENTS",
          "product_type":"PROMOTED_TWEETS",
          "placements":"ALL_ON_TWITTER",
          "bid_amount_local_micro":3210000,
          "entity_status":"PAUSED"
        }
      }
    ]
```

**示例响应[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": [
        {
          "advertiser_user_id": "756201191646691328",
          "name": null,
          "placements": [
            "ALL_ON_TWITTER"
          ],
          "start_time": null,
          "bid_amount_local_micro": 3210000,
          "advertiser_domain": null,
          "target_cpa_local_micro": null,
          "primary_web_event_tag": null,
          "goal": "ENGAGEMENT",
          "daily_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
          "product_type": "PROMOTED_TWEETS",
          "end_time": null,
          "funding_instrument_id": "lygyi",
          "bid_strategy": "MAX",
          "duration_in_days": null,
          "standard_delivery": null,
          "total_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
          "objective": "ENGAGEMENTS",
          "id": "9cqi0",
          "entity_status": "PAUSED",
          "automatic_tweet_promotion": null,
          "frequency_cap": null,
          "android_app_store_identifier": null,
          "categories": [],
          "currency": "USD",
          "pay_by": "ENGAGEMENT",
          "created_at": "2017-07-07T17:42:20Z",
          "ios_app_store_identifier": null,
          "updated_at": "2017-07-07T17:42:20Z",
          "campaign_id": "8yn7m",
          "creative_source": "MANUAL",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ],
      "request": [
        {
          "params": {
            "placements": [
              "ALL_ON_TWITTER"
            ],
            "bid_amount_local_micro": 3210000,
            "product_type": "PROMOTED_TWEETS",
            "objective": "ENGAGEMENTS",
            "entity_status": "PAUSED",
            "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t",
            "campaign_id": "8yn7m"
          },
          "operation_type": "Create"
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="put-accountsaccount_idline_itemsline_item_id">
  #### PUT accounts/:account_id/line_items/:line_item_id[](#put-accounts-account-id-line-items-line-item-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

更新当前账号下指定的 line item。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/line_items/:line_item_id`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| 名称                                                          | 描述                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| :---------------------------------------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需*                                  | 正在使用的账户标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `18ce54d4x5t`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| line&#95;item&#95;id  <br />*必需*                            | 在请求中用于操作的此订单项的引用。<br /><br />Type: 字符串<br /><br />Example: `8v7jo`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| advertiser&#95;domain  <br />*可选*                           | 该广告主的网站域名，不包含协议部分（例如 `http://` 或 `https://`）。<br /><br />**注意**：当该 line item 的投放位置设置为 `PUBLISHER_NETWORK` 时为必需。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`x.com`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| advertiser&#95;user&#95;id  <br />*可选*                      | 用于投放 `PREROLL_VIEWS` 广告的帐号的 Twitter 用户标识符。仅某些客户端应用可以使用此参数。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`312226591`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| android&#95;app&#95;store&#95;identifier  <br />*可选*        | 用于推广应用的 Google Play 商店标识符。<br /><br />**注意**：`APP_INSTALLS` 和 `APP_ENGAGEMENTS` 投放目标要求至少设置一个应用商店标识符——`android_app_store_identifier` 或 `ios_app_store_identifier` 之一。<br /><br />类型：string<br /><br />示例：`com.twitter.android`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| audience&#95;expansion  <br />*可选*                          | 用于通过定向与已定向用户相似的用户来扩大广告活动的触达范围。<br /><br />类型: enum<br /><br />可能的取值: `BROAD`, `DEFINED`, `EXPANDED`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| bid&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*可选*              | 要与此 line item 关联的出价金额。将使用与指定资金工具关联的货币。对于 USD，$5.50 表示为 5500000。<br /><br />**注意**：当 `bid_strategy` 设置为 `MAX` 或 `TARGET` 时为必需。<br /><br />**注意**：只接受大于零的数值。<br /><br />Type: long<br /><br />Example: `140000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| bid&#95;strategy  <br />*可选*                                | 出价机制。<br /><br />`AUTO` 会根据每日预算和广告活动投放周期自动优化出价。<br /><br />`MAX` 用于设置允许的最高出价，当目标设为 `REACH` 或 `FOLLOWERS` 时**不可用**。<br /><br />`TARGET` 会尝试使每日平均出价保持在指定 `bid_amount_local_micro` 的 20% 以内，当目标设为 `REACH` 或 `WEBSITE_CLICKS` 时可用。<br /><br />**注意**：如果设置为 `AUTO`，`bid_amount_local_micro` 将被忽略。<br /><br />**注意**：默认值取决于目标。<br /><br />类型: 枚举（enum）<br /><br />可能的取值: `AUTO`, `MAX`, `TARGET`                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| categories  <br />*可选*                                      | 与该广告主相关的 IAB 类别。参见 [GET iab&#95;categories](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#iab-categories)。<br /><br />**注意**：当 line item 的投放位置设置为 `PUBLISHER_NETWORK` 时为必需。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `IAB3-1`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| duration&#95;in&#95;days  <br />*可选*                        | `frequency_cap` 所适用的时间周期（以天为单位）。<br /><br />类型: int<br /><br />可能的取值: `1`, `7`, `30`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| entity&#95;status  <br />*可选*                               | 投放单状态。<br /><br />类型：枚举<br /><br />可能的取值：`ACTIVE`, `PAUSED`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| end&#95;time  <br />*可选*                                    | 广告项目将停止投放的时间，以 [ISO 8601](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_8601) 格式表示。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `2017-10-05T00:00:00Z`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| frequency&#95;cap  <br />*可选*                               | 向同一用户展示广告的最大次数。<br /><br />**注意**：仅适用于投放目标为 `REACH`、`ENGAGEMENTS`、`VIDEO_VIEWS` 和 `PREROLL_VIEWS` 的广告系列。<br /><br />类型：int<br /><br />示例：`5`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| goal  <br />*可选*                                            | 此 line item 所使用的优化设置。`APP_PURCHASES` 选项可用于 `APP_INSTALL`。`APP_CLICKS` 和 `APP_INSTALLS` 选项可用于 `APP_INSTALL` 和 `APP_ENGAGEMENTS`，并可能要求配合使用受支持的 [MACT 合作伙伴](https://business.x.com/en/help/campaign-setup/create-an-app-installs-or-app-engagement-campaign/mobile-app-conversion-tracking.html)。<br /><br />**注意**：默认值取决于广告目标（objective）。<br /><br />类型: enum<br /><br />可能的取值: `APP_CLICKS`, `APP_INSTALLS`, `APP_PURCHASES`, `ENGAGEMENT`, `FOLLOWERS`, `LINK_CLICKS`, `MAX_REACH`, `PREROLL`, `PREROLL_STARTS`, `REACH_WITH_ENGAGEMENT`, `VIDEO_VIEW`, `VIEW_3S_100PCT`, `VIEW_6S`, `VIEW_15S`, `WEBSITE_CONVERSIONS` |
| ios&#95;app&#95;store&#95;identifier  <br />*可选*            | 用于推广应用的 Apple App Store 标识符的数字部分。<br /><br />**注意**：`APP_INSTALLS` 和 `APP_ENGAGEMENTS` 广告目标要求至少设置一个应用商店标识符——`android_app_store_identifier` 或 `ios_app_store_identifier` 之一。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`333903271`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| 名称  <br />*可选*                                              | 该投放项的名称。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `demo`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| pay&#95;by  <br />*可选*                                      | 用于为该广告项目计费的单位。此设置只能在广告项目使用 `APP_INSTALLS` 目标时进行修改。<br /><br />**注意**：默认的 `pay_by` 会根据广告活动目标和广告项目的出价单位自动设置。<br /><br />`APP_INSTALLS` 目标支持 `APP_CLICK` 和 `IMPRESSION` 两种取值，其中 `IMPRESSION` 为默认值。<br /><br />`LINK_CLICKS` 目标支持 `LINK_CLICK` 和 `IMPRESSION` 两种取值，其中 `IMPRESSION` 为默认值，但在将 `bid_strategy` 设置为 `TARGET` 时不支持 `IMPRESSION`。<br /><br />`SITE_VISITS` 目标支持 `IMPRESSION` 取值。<br /><br />类型: enum<br /><br />可能的取值: `APP_CLICK`, `IMPRESSION`, `LINK_CLICK`                                                                                                                                                    |
| start&#95;time  <br />*可选*                                  | 以 [ISO 8601](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_8601) 格式表示的该 line item 的开始投放时间。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `2017-07-05T00:00:00Z`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| total&#95;budget&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*可选* | 分配给该 line item 的总预算金额。将使用与指定资金工具关联的货币。对于 USD，$37.50 表示为 37500000。<br /><br />类型：long<br /><br />示例：`37500000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| daily&#95;budget&#95;amount&#95;local&#95;micro  <br />*可选* | 要分配给该广告活动的每日预算金额。将使用与指定资金账户关联的货币。对于 USD，$5.50 表示为 5500000。若未提供，该广告活动将根据总预算和投放周期平均花费。仅当父级广告活动的 `budget_optimization` 设置为 `LINE_ITEM` 时可用。<br /><br />**注意**：该值应小于或等于 `total_budget_amount_local_micro`。<br /><br />Type: long<br /><br />Example: `5500000`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

**示例请求[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`PUT https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/line_items/9cqi0?bid_amount_local_micro=140000`

**示例响应[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "line_item_id": "9cqi0",
          "bid_amount_local_micro": 140000,
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "advertiser_user_id": "756201191646691328",
        "name": null,
        "placements": [
          "ALL_ON_TWITTER"
        ],
        "start_time": "2017-07-10T00:00:00Z",
        "bid_amount_local_micro": 140000,
        "advertiser_domain": null,
        "target_cpa_local_micro": null,
        "primary_web_event_tag": null,
        "goal": "ENGAGEMENT",
        "daily_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
        "product_type": "PROMOTED_TWEETS",
        "end_time": null,
        "bid_strategy": "MAX",
        "duration_in_days": null,
        "standard_delivery": null,
        "total_budget_amount_local_micro": null,
        "objective": "ENGAGEMENTS",
        "id": "9cqi0",
        "entity_status": "PAUSED",
        "automatic_tweet_promotion": null,
        "frequency_cap": null,
        "android_app_store_identifier": null,
        "categories": [],
        "currency": "USD",
        "pay_by": "ENGAGEMENT",
        "created_at": "2017-07-07T17:42:20Z",
        "ios_app_store_identifier": null,
        "updated_at": "2022-06-03T23:51:36Z",
        "campaign_id": "8yn7m",
        "creative_source": "MANUAL",
        "deleted": false
      }
    }
```

<div id="delete-accountsaccount_idline_itemsline_item_id">
  #### DELETE accounts/:account_id/line_items/:line_item_id[](#delete-accounts-account-id-line-items-line-item-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

删除当前账户下指定的 line item。

**注意**：删除 line item 后无法恢复，之后再次尝试删除该资源将返回 HTTP 404。

**注意**：当一个 line item 被删除后，只有在请求中指定 `with_deleted=true` 时，其子级 promoted&#95;tweets 才会在 GET accounts/:account&#95;id/promoted&#95;tweets 和 GET accounts/:account&#95;id/promoted&#95;tweets/:promoted&#95;tweet&#95;id 端点的返回结果中出现。请注意，这些 promoted&#95;tweets 实际上并未被删除（响应中 `"deleted": false`）。我们不会进行级联删除。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/line_items/:line_item_id`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                   | Description                                                                                                                                                                       |
| :------------------------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*       | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是所有 Advertiser API 请求（不包括 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts)）的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`18ce54d4x5t` |
| line&#95;item&#95;id  <br />*required* | 在请求中要操作的 line item 的引用。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`9f2ix`                                                                                                                   |

**请求示例[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`DELETE https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/line_items/9f2ix`

**响应示例[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": {
        "bid_strategy": "MAX",
        "advertiser_user_id": "756201191646691328",
        "name": "无标题",
        "placements": [],
        "start_time": null,
        "bid_amount_local_micro": 100000,
        "advertiser_domain": null,
        "target_cpa_local_micro": null,
        "primary_web_event_tag": null,
        "pay_by": "ENGAGEMENT",
        "product_type": "PROMOTED_TWEETS",
```

<div id="line-item-curated-categories">
  ### 推广单元精选类别
</div>

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">

关于用法的更多详细信息，请参阅[视频观看量前贴片目标指南](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#video-views-preroll-objective)。

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idline_item_curated_categories">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/line_item_curated_categories[](#get-accounts-account-id-line-item-curated-categories "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索与当前账户关联的部分或全部 line item curated categories 的详细信息。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/line_item_curated_categories`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                 | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| :----------------------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需*           | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `18ce54d4x5t`                               |
| count  <br />*可选*                    | 指定每个请求尝试检索的记录数量。<br /><br />类型: int<br /><br />默认值: `200`  <br />最小值、最大值: `1`、`1000`                                                                                                                            |
| cursor  <br />*可选*                   | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。更多信息参见 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `8x7v00oow`                                                                                                      |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*可选*              | 按受支持的属性进行升序或降序排序。更多信息参见 [Sorting](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `created_at-asc`                                                                                                  |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*可选*         | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />类型: boolean<br /><br />默认值: `false`  <br />可能的取值: `true`、`false`                                                                                                                       |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*可选* | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**注意**: 此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**注意**: 包含 `total_count` 的请求将具有更低的速率限制，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />类型: boolean<br /><br />默认值: `false`  <br />可能的取值: `true`、`false` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/abc1/line_item_curated_categories`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```josn
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "account_id": "abc1"
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "line_item_id": "by5pw",
          "curated_category_id": "7op29tp2jzeo",
          "id": "1",
          "created_at": "2018-06-29T04:19:53Z",
          "updated_at": "2018-06-29T04:19:53Z",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idline_item_curated_categoriesline_item_curated_category_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/line_item_curated_categories/:line_item_curated_category_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-line-item-curated-categories-line-item-curated-category-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

获取与当前账户关联的特定 line item 精选分类的详细信息。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                                            | Description                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| :-------------------------------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*                                | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已通过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| line&#95;item&#95;curated&#95;category&#95;id  <br />*required* | 你在请求中要操作的 line item 精选分类的引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `43853bhii885`                                                                                                           |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*                              | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                        |

```json
        "deleted": false
      }
    }
```

<div id="post-accountsaccount_idline_item_curated_categories">

| Name                                          | Description                                                                                                                                                                    |
| :-------------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />`required`              | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`18ce54d4x5t` |
| curated&#95;category&#95;id  <br />`required` | 在请求中要操作的精选分类实体的引用。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`10miy`                                                                                                                     |

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idmedia_creatives">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/media_creatives[](#get-accounts-account-id-media-creatives "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

检索与当前账户关联的部分或全部媒体创意的详细信息。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/media_creatives`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                                   | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| :------------------------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需*             | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `18ce54d4x5t`                              |
| campaign&#95;id  <br />*可选*            | 将响应限定为仅包含与指定广告活动关联的媒体创意。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `8gdx6`                                                                                                                                              |
| count  <br />*可选*                      | 指定每个独立请求尝试检索的记录数。<br /><br />类型: int<br /><br />默认值: `200`  <br />最小值与最大值: `1`, `1000`                                                                                                                          |
| cursor  <br />*可选*                     | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。更多信息参见 [分页](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `8x7v00oow`                                                                                                              |
| line&#95;item&#95;ids  <br />*可选*      | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应限定为仅包含与指定广告单元关联的媒体创意。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `8v7jo`                                                                                                               |
| media&#95;creative&#95;ids  <br />*可选* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应限定为仅包含所需的媒体创意。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `1bzq3`                                                                                                                      |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*可选*                | 按受支持的属性进行升序或降序排序。更多信息参见 [排序](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `created_at-asc`                                                                                                       |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*可选*           | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />类型: boolean<br /><br />默认值: `false`  <br />可能的取值: `true`, `false`                                                                                                                      |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*可选*   | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**注意**: 此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**注意**: 包含 `total_count` 的请求具有更低的速率限制，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />类型: boolean<br /><br />默认值: `false`  <br />可能的取值: `true`, `false` |

**示例请求[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/media_creatives?media_creative_ids=1bzq3`

**示例响应[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t",
          "media_creative_ids": [
            "1bzq3"
          ]
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "line_item_id": "8v7jo",
          "landing_url": "https://dev.x.com",
          "creative_type": "INTERSTITIAL_LANDSCAPE_TABLET",
          "id": "1bzq3",
          "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
          "created_at": "2017-07-05T06:00:42Z",
          "account_media_id": "10miy",
          "updated_at": "2019-01-11T20:21:26Z",
          "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="post-accountsaccount_idmedia_creatives">
  #### POST accounts/:account_id/media_creatives[](#post-accounts-account-id-media-creatives "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

将 [account media](/zh/x-ads-api/creatives#account-media) 对象与指定的 line item 关联。

使用此端点在 Twitter Audience Platform 上推广流内广告（当 account media 的 `creative_type` 为 `PREROLL` 时）或图片广告（例如 `BANNER` 或 `INTERSTITIAL`）。

**注意**：要将媒体资产添加到 Account Media 资源，请使用 [POST accounts/:account&#95;id/media&#95;library](/zh/x-ads-api/creatives#account-media) 端点。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/media_creatives`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                        | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| :------------------------------------------ | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />`required`            | 被使用账号的标识符。出现在该资源的路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账号必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t`                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| account&#95;media&#95;id  <br />`required`  | 在请求中要操作的 account media 实体的引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `10miy`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| line&#95;item&#95;id  <br />`required`      | 在请求中要操作的 line item 的引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8v7jo`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| landing&#95;url  <br />`sometimes required` | 用于将用户引导至目标网站的 URL。仅应与 TAP 图片（或“展示创意”）一起使用。如果与 preroll 资产一起使用，此值将被忽略。要将 URL 与 preroll 资产关联，请使用 [POST accounts/:account&#95;id/preroll&#95;call&#95;to&#95;actions](/zh/x-ads-api/creatives#post-accounts-account-id-preroll-call-to-actions) 端点。<br /><br />**注意**：当 line item 的 objective 设置为 `WEBSITE_CLICKS` 时为必需。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `https://blog.x.com/` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`POST https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/media_creatives?line_item_id=8v7jo&account_media_id=10miy`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "line_item_id": "8v7jo",
          "account_media_id": "10miy",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "8v7jo",
        "landing_url": "https://dev.x.com",
        "creative_type": "INTERSTITIAL_LANDSCAPE_TABLET",
        "id": "1bzq3",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-07-05T06:00:42Z",
        "account_media_id": "10miy",
        "updated_at": "2019-01-11T20:21:26Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "deleted": false
      }
    }
```

<div id="delete-accountsaccount_idmedia_creativesmedia_creative_id">
  #### DELETE accounts/:account_id/media_creatives/:media_creative_id[](#delete-accounts-account-id-media-creatives-media-creative-id "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

删除当前账户下指定的媒体创意。

| media&#95;creative&#95;id  <br />*必需* | 在请求中要操作的媒体创意的标识符。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `1bzq3`                                                                                                                       |

**请求示例[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`DELETE https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/media_creatives/1bzq3`

**响应示例[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "request": {

          "media_creative_id": "1bzq3",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "8v7jo",
        "landing_url": "https://dev.x.com",
        "creative_type": "INTERSTITIAL_LANDSCAPE_TABLET",
        "id": "1bzq3",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-07-05T06:00:42Z",
        "account_media_id": "10miy",
        "updated_at": "2021-04-16T21:02:55Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "deleted": true
      }
    }
```

### 推广账号

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  在 Postman 中运行
</Button>

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idpromoted_accounts">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts[](#get-accounts-account-id-promoted-accounts "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索当前账户下一个或多个推广线项所关联的部分或全部推广账号的详细信息。

使用 [GET users/lookup](https://developer.x.com/en/docs/x-api/v1/accounts-and-users/follow-search-get-users/api-reference/get-users-lookup) 来获取由响应中的 `user_id` 标识的用户账号的用户数据。

如果指定的线项均未配置包含推广账号，将返回 HTTP 400 响应。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                           | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| :--------------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*               | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t`                                     |
| count  <br />*optional*                        | 指定每个请求尝试检索的记录数。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `200`  <br />Min, Max: 最小值 `1`，最大值 `1000`                                                                                                                             |
| cursor  <br />*optional*                       | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。更多信息参见 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8x7v00oow`                                                                                                           |
| line&#95;item&#95;ids  <br />*optional*        | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应范围限定为仅包含与指定线项关联的推广账号。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `9bpb2`                                                                                                                    |
| promoted&#95;account&#95;ids  <br />*optional* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应范围限定为仅包含所需的推广账号。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `19pl2`                                                                                                                         |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*optional*                  | 按受支持的属性以升序或降序排序。更多信息参见 [Sorting](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `created_at-asc`                                                                                                        |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*             | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                                                     |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*optional*     | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**注意**：此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**注意**：包含 `total_count` 的请求会具有更低的速率限制，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_accounts?promoted_account_ids=19pl2`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promoted_account_ids": [
            "19pl2"
          ],
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "line_item_id": "9bpb2",
          "user_id": "756201191646691328",
          "id": "19pl2",
          "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
          "created_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
          "updated_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
          "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idpromoted_accountspromoted_account_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts/:promoted_account_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-promoted-accounts-promoted-account-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索当前账户下与某个订单项关联的特定账户引用。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts/:promoted_account_id`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                          | Description                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| :-------------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*              | 关联账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| promoted&#95;account&#95;id  <br />*required* | 在请求中所操作的推广账户引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `19pl2`                                                                                                                         |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*            | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                  |

**请求示例[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_accounts/19pl2`

**响应示例[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promoted_account_id": "19pl2",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "9bpb2",
        "user_id": "756201191646691328",
        "id": "19pl2",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "deleted": false
      }
    }
```

<div id="post-accountsaccount_idpromoted_accounts">
  #### POST accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts[](#post-accounts-account-id-promoted-accounts "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

将一个账号（`user_id`）与指定的广告组（line item）关联。

如果指定的广告组未配置为可与 Promoted Accounts 关联，将返回 HTTP 400 `INCOMPATIBLE_LINE_ITEM` 错误。如果指定的用户不符合推广资格，将返回 HTTP 400 错误，并且不会推广任何用户。如果提供的用户已经被推广，则会忽略该请求。

有关 Promoted Accounts 的更多信息，请参阅我们的[广告活动管理](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#advertiser-api)页面。

**注意**：无法通过 PUT 更新 promoted accounts 实体。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                   | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| :------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*       | 所使用账号的标识符。出现在资源路径中，通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账号必须与已授权用户关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`18ce54d4x5t`                                                             |
| line&#95;item&#95;id  <br />*required* | 你在请求中操作的广告组（line item）的引用。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`9bpb2`                                                                                                                                                                         |
| user&#95;id  <br />*required*          | 你在请求中操作的用户的引用。使用 [GET users/lookup](https://developer.x.com/en/docs/x-api/v1/accounts-and-users/follow-search-get-users/api-reference/get-users-lookup) 根据 screen name 获取用户 id。<br /><br />类型：长整型（long）<br /><br />示例：`756201191646691328` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`POST https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_accounts?line_item_id=9bpb2&user_id=756201191646691328`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "9bpb2",
        "user_id": "756201191646691328",
        "id": "19pl2",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "deleted": false
      },
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "user_id": "756201191646691328",
          "line_item_id": "9bpb2",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="delete-accountsaccount_idpromoted_accountspromoted_account_id">
  #### DELETE accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts/:promoted_account_id[](#delete-accounts-account-id-promoted-accounts-promoted-account-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

将某个账户与指定的广告组（line item）解除关联。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_accounts/:promoted_account_id`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                          | Description                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| :-------------------------------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*              | 用于投放的账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是所有 Advertiser API 请求（[GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 除外）的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已通过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| promoted&#95;account&#95;id  <br />*required* | 与广告组（line item）关联的 Promoted Account 实例的标识符。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `19pl2`                                                                                                     |

**示例请求[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`DELETE https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_accounts/19pl2`

**示例响应[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "9bpb2",
        "user_id": "756201191646691328",
        "id": "19pl2",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-07-05T05:54:13Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-08-23T18:53:15Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "deleted": true
      },
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promoted_account_id": "19pl2",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="promoted-tweets">
  ### 推广推文
</div>

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  在 Postman 中运行
</Button>

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idpromoted_tweets">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets[](#get-accounts-account-id-promoted-tweets "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索与当前账户下广告组关联的推文引用。

使用 [GET accounts/:account&#95;id/tweets](/zh/x-ads-api/creatives#get-accounts-account-id-tweets) 端点来获取推文对象。对于每个 promoted&#95;tweets 对象，使用其 `tweet_id` 值。

**注意**：当父级广告组被删除时，仅当在请求中指定了 `with_deleted=true` 时才会返回对应的 promoted&#95;tweets。不过，这些 promoted&#95;tweets 实际上并未被删除（响应中 `"deleted": false`）。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                   | Description                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| :------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需*             | 所使用的账户标识符。出现在资源路径中，通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `18ce54d4x5t`                         |
| count  <br />*可选*                      | 指定每个独立请求尝试检索的记录数量。<br /><br />类型: 整数<br /><br />默认值: `200`  <br />最小值、最大值: `1`, `1000`                                                                                                                  |
| cursor  <br />*可选*                     | 指定用于获取下一页结果的游标。更多信息请参见[分页](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `8x7v00oow`                                                                                                      |
| line&#95;item&#95;ids  <br />*可选*      | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应限定为仅包含与特定广告组关联的推文。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `96uzp`                                                                                                          |
| promoted&#95;tweet&#95;ids  <br />*可选* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应限定为仅包含所需的推广推文。最多可提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `1efwlo`                                                                                                             |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*可选*                | 按受支持的属性进行升序或降序排序。更多信息请参见[排序](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />类型: 字符串<br /><br />示例: `created_at-asc`                                                                                               |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*可选*           | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />类型: 布尔值<br /><br />默认值: `false`  <br />可能的取值: `true`, `false`                                                                                                                  |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*可选*   | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**注意**：此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**注意**：包含 `total_count` 的请求其速率限制更低，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />类型: 布尔值<br /><br />默认值: `false`  <br />可能的取值: `true`, `false` |

**示例请求[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_tweets?promoted_tweet_ids=1efwlo`

**示例响应[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promoted_tweet_ids": [
            "1efwlo"
          ],
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "line_item_id": "96uzp",
          "id": "1efwlo",
          "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
          "created_at": "2017-06-29T05:06:57Z",
          "updated_at": "2017-06-29T05:08:46Z",
          "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
          "tweet_id": "880290790664060928",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idpromoted_tweetspromoted_tweet_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets/:promoted_tweet_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-promoted-tweets-promoted-tweet-id "指向此标题的永久链接")
</div>

检索当前账户下某个订单项关联的特定推文引用。

**注意**：当父级订单项被删除时，仅当在请求中指定 `with_deleted=true` 时才会返回 `promoted_tweets`。这些 `promoted_tweets` 实际上并未被删除（响应中 `"deleted": false`）。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "指向此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets/:promoted_tweet_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "指向此标题的永久链接")**

| Name                                  | Description                                                                                                                                                                        |
| :------------------------------------ | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需*            | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 以外所有 Advertiser API 请求的一项必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`18ce54d4x5t` |
| promoted&#95;tweet&#95;id  <br />*必需* | 在请求中你要操作的推广推文引用。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`1efwlo`                                                                                                                          |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*可选*          | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />类型：布尔值<br /><br />默认值：`false`  <br />可能的取值：`true`, `false`                                                                                                |

**Example Request[](#example-request "指向此标题的永久链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_tweets/1efwlo`

**Example Response[](#example-response "指向此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promoted_tweet_id": "1efwlo",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "96uzp",
        "id": "1efwlo",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-06-29T05:06:57Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-06-29T05:08:46Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "tweet_id": "880290790664060928",
        "deleted": false
      }
    }
```

<div id="post-accountsaccount_idpromoted_tweets">
  #### POST accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets[](#post-accounts-account-id-promoted-tweets "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

将一个或多个推文关联到指定的 line item。并非所有推文都适合推广，这取决于广告系列目标。请参阅[基于目标的广告系列](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#objective-based-campaigns)以获取更多信息。

当使用 `PROMOTED_ACCOUNT` 产品类型时，将推文与 `line_item` 关联会在移动端时间线中增加投放位置，此外还会包含标准的 `PROMOTED_ACCOUNT` 投放位置。

**注意**：无法对已推广的推文实体执行更新（PUT）操作。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| 名称                               | 描述                                                                                                                                                                       |
| :------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需*       | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是所有广告主 API 请求（[GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 除外）的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`18ce54d4x5t` |
| line&#95;item&#95;id  <br />*必需* | 你在此请求中操作的 line item 的引用。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`8v7jo`                                                                                                         |
| tweet&#95;ids  <br />*必需*        | 与特定推文对应的、以逗号分隔的标识符列表。最多可以提供 50 个 ID。<br /><br />类型：long<br /><br />示例：`822333526255120384`                                                                               |

**示例请求[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`POST https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_tweets?line_item_id=8v7jo&tweet_ids=822333526255120384`

**示例响应[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": [
        {
          "line_item_id": "8v7jo",
          "id": "1e8i2k",
          "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
          "created_at": "2017-06-24T04:21:36Z",
          "updated_at": "2017-06-24T04:21:36Z",
          "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
          "tweet_id": "822333526255120384",
          "deleted": false
        }
      ],
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "line_item_id": "8v7jo",
          "tweet_ids": [
            822333526255120384
          ],
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "total_count": 1
    }
```

<div id="delete-accountsaccount_idpromoted_tweetspromoted_tweet_id">
  #### DELETE accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets/:promoted_tweet_id[](#delete-accounts-account-id-promoted-tweets-promoted-tweet-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

将某条推文与指定的 line item 解除关联。

**注意**：已删除的 `promoted_tweets` 实体将在 ads.x.com 界面中显示为“Paused”。同样地，在界面中执行 “pausing” 操作也会将该推文与其 line item 解除关联。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promoted_tweets/:promoted_tweet_id`

**参数[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| 名称                                    | 描述                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| :------------------------------------ | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需*            | 用于投放账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是所有 Advertiser API 请求（[GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 除外）的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`18ce54d4x5t`                                          |
| promoted&#95;tweet&#95;id  <br />*必需* | 标识与某个 line item 关联的 Promoted 推文实例。该值来自 [GET accounts/:account&#95;id/promoted&#95;tweets](#get-accounts-account-id-promoted-tweets) 响应项中的 `id` 字段，而不是相关推文本身的 `tweet_id`。在资源路径中传入。<br /><br />类型：字符串<br /><br />示例：`1gp8a5` |

**请求示例[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`DELETE https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promoted_tweets/1gp8a5`

**响应示例[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "data": {
        "line_item_id": "9pl99",
        "id": "1gp8a5",
        "entity_status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2017-08-17T17:02:21Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-08-18T06:43:48Z",
        "approval_status": "ACCEPTED",
        "tweet_id": "844796297743757315",
        "deleted": true
      },
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promoted_tweet_id": "1gp8a5",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      }
    }
```

<div id="promotable-users">
  ### 可推广用户
</div>

<Button href="https://app.getpostman.com/run-collection/1d12b9fc623b8e149f87">
  在 Postman 中运行
</Button>

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idpromotable_users">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/promotable_users[](#get-accounts-account-id-promotable-users "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索与当前账号关联的部分或全部可投放用户的详细信息。

可投放用户类型为 `FULL` 或 `RETWEETS_ONLY`。这将控制该账号允许投放的内容类型。广告主必须获得权限才能投放其他用户的内容，并联系 Twitter 将这些用户作为 `RETWEETS_ONLY` 类型的可投放用户添加到你的账号中。

在权限设置正确的情况下，你可以向推广产品端点发出请求，直接引用你希望推广的推文的 Tweet ID。你可以使用 [POST accounts/:account&#95;id/promoted-tweets](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#promoted-tweets) 端点来推广已发布的推文，以及使用 [POST accounts/:account&#95;id/scheduled-promoted-tweets](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#promoted-tweets) 端点来推广其他 Twitter Ads 账号的预定推文。

你不必转推目标推文。使用此方法推广推文时，返回的 `tweet_id` 将不同于提供的 Tweet ID。在后台，该推文会以 nullcast 方式被转推，然后再进行投放。返回的 `tweet_id` 对应的是这条新的推文。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promotable_users`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| 名称 | 描述 |
| :--- | :--- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需* | 所使用账号的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账号必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| count  <br />*可选* | 指定每个独立请求尝试检索的记录数量。<br /><br />Type: int<br /><br />Default: `200`  <br />Min, Max: `1`, `1000` |
| cursor  <br />*可选* | 指定一个游标以获取下一页结果。更多信息请参阅 [Pagination](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `8x7v00oow` |
| promotable&#95;user&#95;ids  <br />*可选* | 通过指定以逗号分隔的标识符列表，将响应范围限定为所需的可投放用户。最多可以提供 200 个 ID。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `l310s` |
| sort&#95;by  <br />*可选* | 按受支持的属性进行升序或降序排序。更多信息请参阅 [Sorting](/zh/x-ads-api/introduction)。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `created_at-asc` |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*可选* | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |
| with&#95;total&#95;count  <br />*可选* | 在响应中包含 `total_count` 属性。<br /><br />**注意**：此参数与 `cursor` 互斥。<br /><br />**注意**：包含 `total_count` 的请求将具有较低的速率限制，目前设置为每 15 分钟 200 次。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false` |

**示例请求[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promotable_users?promotable_user_ids=l310s`

**示例响应[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promotable_user_ids": [
            "l310s"
          ],
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "next_cursor": null,
      "data": [
        {
          "user_id": "756201191646691328",
          "id": "l310s",
          "created_at": "2016-07-21T22:42:09Z",
          "updated_at": "2016-07-21T22:42:09Z",
          "deleted": false,
          "promotable_user_type": "FULL"
        }
      ]
    }
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idpromotable_userspromotable_user_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/promotable_users/:promotable_user_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-promotable-users-promotable-user-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

检索与当前账号关联的特定可推广用户。

可推广用户类型可以是 `FULL` 或 `RETWEETS_ONLY`。它决定该账号允许推广的内容类型。

广告主必须获得权限才能推广其他用户的内容。在权限设置正确的前提下，可以向推广产品相关的端点发起请求，直接引用你想推广的推文的 Tweet ID。

你不必转推目标推文。使用这种方式推广推文时，返回的 `tweet_id` 将不同于你提供的 Tweet ID。在后台，该推文会被转推为一次 nullcast 推文，然后再进行推广。返回的 `tweet_id` 对应的是这条新的推文。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/:account_id/promotable_users/:promotable_user_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name                                         | Description                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| :------------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required*             | 被使用账号的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账号必须与已认证用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| promotable&#95;user&#95;id  <br />*optional* | 你在请求中要操作的可推广用户引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `l310s`                                                                                                                        |
| with&#95;deleted  <br />*optional*           | 在请求中包含已删除的结果。<br /><br />Type: boolean<br /><br />Default: `false`  <br />Possible values: `true`, `false`                                                                                   |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/12/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/promotable_users/l310s`

**Example Response[](#example-response "Permalink to this headline")**

```json
    {
      "request": {
        "params": {
          "promotable_user_id": "l310s",
          "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
        }
      },
      "data": {
        "user_id": "2417045708",
        "id": "l310s",
        "created_at": "2017-03-10T17:51:24Z",
        "updated_at": "2017-03-10T17:51:24Z",
        "deleted": false,
        "promotable_user_type": "RETWEETS_ONLY"
      }
    }
```

<div id="recommendations">
  ### 推荐
</div>

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idrecommendations">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/recommendations[](#get-accounts-account-id-recommendations "此标题的永久链接")
</div>

状态：*封闭 Beta*

检索与此广告账户关联的广告系列推荐。目前，每个资金工具（funding instrument）最多返回 1 条推荐。

**资源 URL[](#resource-url "此标题的永久链接")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/5/accounts/:account_id/recommendations`

**参数[](#parameters "此标题的永久链接")**

| 名称                         | 描述                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| :------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*必需* | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源的路径中，并且通常是所有 Advertiser API 请求（[GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 除外）的必需参数。指定的账户必须与已通过身份验证的用户相关联。<br /><br />类型: string<br /><br />示例: `18ce54d4x5t` |

**示例请求[](#example-request "此标题的永久链接")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/5/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/recommendations`

**示例响应[](#example-response "此标题的永久链接")**

```json
    "request": {
      "params": {
        "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t"
      }
    },
    "total_count": 1,
    "data": [
      {
        "funding_instrument_id": "gpvzb",
        "id": "62ce8zza1q0w",
        "account_id": "18ce54d4x5t",
        "status": "PENDING",
        "message": "Recommendation for testing",
        "created_at": "2016-11-14T23:07:54Z",
        "updated_at": "2016-11-14T23:07:54Z"
      }
    ]
```

<div id="get-accountsaccount_idrecommendationsrecommendation_id">
  #### GET accounts/:account_id/recommendations/:recommendation_id[](#get-accounts-account-id-recommendations-recommendation-id "Permalink to this headline")
</div>

状态：*封闭 Beta 阶段*

检索与此广告账户关联的特定广告系列推荐。

广告系列推荐包含一整套针对广告系列结构的修改建议，并以对象树的形式表示。该响应树旨在与 Batch API 端点配合使用，但也可以根据需要映射到单个更新端点（POST 对应 Create，PUT 对应 Update，DELETE 对应 Delete）。

**Resource URL[](#resource-url "Permalink to this headline")**

`https://ads-api.x.com/5/accounts/:account_id/recommendations/:recommendation_id`

**Parameters[](#parameters "Permalink to this headline")**

| Name | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| account&#95;id  <br />*required* | 所使用账户的标识符。出现在资源路径中，并且通常是除 [GET accounts](/zh/x-ads-api/campaign-management#get-accounts) 之外所有 Advertiser API 请求的必需参数。指定的账户必须与经过身份验证的用户关联。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `18ce54d4x5t` |
| recommendation&#95;id  <br />*required* | 你在此请求中要操作的推荐的 ID 引用。<br /><br />Type: string<br /><br />Example: `62ce8zza1q0w` |

**Example Request[](#example-request "Permalink to this headline")**

`GET https://ads-api.x.com/5/accounts/18ce54d4x5t/recommendations/62ce8zza1q0w`
